- **prompt-optimizer**: Use this agent when you need to improve, refine, or optimize prompts for AI systems, LLMs, or other automated tools. - **proposal-reviewer**: Reviews one OpenSpec change before any of its code is written, in one of three roles - critic (unmade product decisions, rollout and deploy-day risks, missed callers), readiness (whether each task group's verification really tests the change, seed and fixtures, group order) or codebase (every file, function, schema, route and dependency the change names, checked against the code and the open changes it depends on). Started by the proposal-review skill; changes no code and writes its findings to a report file. ([namnhatpham1995/hackathon-stuttgart-2026](https://github.com/namnhatpham1995/hackathon-stuttgart-2026/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/proposal-reviewer.md))
- **proposal-reviewer**: Critically review a project proposal made with the project-proposal skill and write findings to an approval page. Use when the user asks to review, check, audit or pressure-test a proposal or plan, or at milestones (summary agreed, experiments and budget drafted, draft complete). Flags anything that is not simple enough to start, weak or missing infrastructure, infeasible or unjustified experiments, unclear or jargon-heavy writing, unsupported claims, and gaps in the threat model. Read-only on the proposal itself. ([ncrispino/skills](https://github.com/ncrispino/skills/blob/HEAD/project-proposal/agents/proposal-reviewer.md))
- **proposal-reviewer**: Reviews the proposal a run wrote against the checking-proposals check list, the person's words and the sources it cites, read-only, and reports findings as text.
